# META
~~~ini
description=Indexed member access reports unresolved index operands, missing members and ambiguous member scopes, rather than silently dropping an index or choosing a candidate.
type=file
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package IndexedErrors {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    attribute def Vertex { attribute coordinate : Real; }
    attribute def Alternative { attribute coordinate : Real; }
    attribute def Edge { attribute vertices : Vertex[2]; }
    calc def MissingIndex {
        in edges : Edge[0..*];
        return result : Vertex[2] = edges#(missing).vertices;
    }
    calc def MissingMember {
        in edges : Edge[0..*];
        in i : Integer;
        return result : Real = edges#(i).missing;
    }
    calc def AmbiguousMember {
        attribute alternatives : Vertex, Alternative[0..*];
        return result : Real = alternatives#(1).coordinate;
    }
}
~~~
